Now I have all the files read. Let me verify a few more things:

1. The `_errCount` and `_errBuf` are defined in metrics.js (lines 33-34), but referenced in utils.js's `swallow` function (lines 25, 27, 29, 30). The comment in utils.js explicitly notes this is a "前向引用" (forward reference). The question is whether this is safe. Since both are in the same IIFE closure, and `var` declarations are hoisted, the variables ARE hoisted to the top of the IIFE. The `swallow` function only references them at runtime (when called), by which point metrics.js has been evaluated and assigned the initial values `[]` and `0`. BUT - the critical issue is: if `swallow` is called BEFORE metrics.js is evaluated (i.e., during the evaluation of utils.js itself or any module between utils.js and metrics.js), then `_errCount` and `_errBuf` would be `undefined` (hoisted but not yet assigned). The comment in utils.js warns about NOT calling `dbg()` at top level because DEBUG is defined later. Similarly, `swallow` at top level would fail because `_errCount++` on undefined would throw... actually `_errCount++` where _errCount is undefined throws `ReferenceError`? No - if it's declared with `var` it's hoisted, so `_errCount` would be `undefined`, and `undefined++` → `NaN`, not an error. And `_errBuf.push` where `_errBuf` is `undefined` → TypeError. So if swallow is called at top-level before metrics.js evaluates, it would throw. But the code is careful not to call swallow at top level. This is a latent risk though.

Wait, let me re-read. The toolkit object in utils.js (line 283) exposes `recentErrors` and `errorCount`. These are defined in metrics.js. If metrics.js loads AFTER utils.js, then at the time utils.js evaluates the toolkit assignment (lines 276-285), `recentErrors` and `errorCount` are function declarations that are hoisted within the IIFE. Function declarations ARE hoisted with their body, so `recentErrors` and `errorCount` would be available even if metrics.js is textually after utils.js. So that's actually fine for function declarations.

But `_errCount` and `_errBuf` are `var` declarations - they're hoisted but NOT initialized until metrics.js executes. So the risk is only if `swallow` is called before metrics.js executes. The comment acknowledges this.

2. Let me check the `_attachStore` reference in lifecycle.js line 75. It's defined in targeting.js line 51. lifecycle.js uses `_attachStore ? _attachStore.value : undefined`. If targeting.js hasn't executed yet or `_attachStore` is still null, this would be `null ? ... : undefined` → `undefined`. That's fine because `_attachStore` is declared with `var _attachStore = null` in targeting.js, so it's hoisted and initialized to undefined, then set to null when targeting.js runs. Actually wait - `var _attachStore = null` hoists the declaration (to undefined) but the `= null` assignment only happens when targeting.js executes. So before targeting.js executes, `_attachStore` is `undefined`. In lifecycle.js, `_attachStore ? _attachStore.value : undefined` - undefined is falsy, so returns undefined. That's safe. But there's no `typeof` guard - if targeting.js failed to load for some reason... actually it's in the same IIFE so it will always be declared. OK.

But wait - lifecycle.js line 75 references `_attachStore` WITHOUT a typeof guard. The code at line 72 checks `_attachHooked && typeof siteAttachmentsKey === 'function'` but doesn't guard `_attachStore`. If `_attachStore` is somehow not defined (e.g., targeting.js not loaded), it would throw ReferenceError. But since they're in the same IIFE, `_attachStore` is always declared (hoisted). So it's safe in practice, but it's inconsistent with the defensive style used elsewhere.

让我验证几个关键疑点：`_pqQuery` / `_pqQueryAll` 的定义位置，以及 `_mo` 的赋值细节。
Now I have enough context. Let me verify a few more critical things before producing the final analysis:

1. The `cxState` is a WeakMap (confirmed) - it returns `{}` for nodes without state. So `cxState(v).foo` never throws for non-null v - it just returns undefined. But for `null` it would throw. Let me check if there are cases where v could be null/undefined when cxState is called.

2. The `_mo` MutationObserver is defined as `var _mo = null;` at line 369 in dom.js and assigned with `try { _mo = new MutationObserver(...).observe(...) }`. The issue here is that `.observe()` returns `undefined`, not the observer instance. So `_mo = new MutationObserver(...).observe(...)` would assign `undefined` to `_mo`! Wait, let me re-read it:

```javascript
var _mo = null;   // MutationObserver 实例（审查 JS1-2：页面卸载时断开，避免孤立回调滞留）
try {
    _mo = new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
      ...
    }).observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
} catch (e) { swallow(e); }
```

YES! This is a real bug. `_mo = new MutationObserver(fn).observe(...)` — the `.observe()` method returns `undefined`, so `_mo` is set to `undefined`, not the observer instance. Then in lifecycle.js:
```javascript
try { if (_mo && _mo.disconnect) _mo.disconnect(); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
```
Since `_mo` is `undefined`, the `if (_mo && ...)` is always false, so `disconnect()` is NEVER called! This is the classic MutationObserver assignment bug the user is asking about.

3. The `nearEnd` function in playback.js — it calls `v.ended` and `v.duration` / `v.currentTime` without null checks. If v is null (which shouldn't happen given callers check), it would throw. But the try/catch handles it. The issue is more subtle.

4. Let me check `isRebuildFinished(v)` in dedup.js - it calls `videoSrcOf(v)` and `videoIframeSrcsOf(v)`. `videoSrcOf` has try/catch. `videoIframeSrcsOf` calls `cxState(v).hostSigs` — if v is null, cxState(null) would throw because WeakMap.get(null) actually returns undefined (WeakMap allows null as key lookup but throws for non-objects). Actually WeakMap.get(null) returns undefined, WeakMap.get(undefined) returns undefined. But `cxState(null)` — let me check: `videoState.get(null)` — actually this throws "Invalid value used as weak map key" for non-object/non-null-prototype values. Wait, actually WeakMap.get(null) returns undefined (null is allowed as a lookup key but won't find anything). WeakMap.get(undefined) also returns undefined. So `cxState(null)` would call `videoState.get(null)` which returns undefined, then `s = {}` and `videoState.set(null, {})` which THROWS "Invalid value used in weak map" because null is not a valid key for set!

Actually, let me verify: WeakMap.prototype.set with null throws TypeError. WeakMap.prototype.get with null... let me think. The spec says get/set require the key to be an object or non-registered symbol. null is not an object. So both get and set with null throw? Actually no — get is more lenient in some implementations but per spec it should also throw. Hmm, but in practice WeakMap.get(null) returns undefined in V8. Let me not go down this rabbit hole; the practical concern is whether null v reaches these functions. Looking at callers:
- `isRebuildFinished(v)` is called in dom.js `_ovRebuild(v)` which checks `v.ended` first (so v is not null), and in `_ovEnforce` event handlers which check `v.ended` etc.
- Actually in the canplay/waiting/stalled handlers, they call `isRebuildFinished(v)` where v is captured in closure — v is the video element, should not be null.

But the user specifically asks about `nearEnd/isRebuildFinished 对 null 视频`. Let me look at `nearEnd`:
```javascript
function nearEnd(v) {
    try {
      if (v.ended) return false;
      if (isFinite(v.duration) && v.duration > 0 && v.currentTime >= v.duration - CONFIG.END_RELEASE_SEC) return true;
    } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
    return false;
}
```
If v is null, `v.ended` throws inside try/catch, swallowed, returns false. OK so that's handled. But `CONFIG.END_RELEASE_SEC` — if CONFIG is undefined this throws ReferenceError outside the try? No, `v.ended` is evaluated first inside try. Actually the whole body is in try. So it's fine.

Wait, actually there's a subtle issue: `isFinite(v.duration)` — if v.duration is NaN (no metadata loaded), isFinite(NaN) is false, so it skips. Good. But if v.duration is Infinity (live stream), `v.duration > 0` is true, and `v.currentTime >= Infinity - 15` is `currentTime >= Infinity` which is false unless currentTime is Infinity. So nearEnd returns false for live streams. OK.

Let me also check the `_ovNearEnd` in dom.js line 79:
```javascript
dbg('进度到底（剩 ' + Math.max(0, (v.duration - v.currentTime)).toFixed(0) + 's）：已关闭续播');
```

Now let me look at the auto-next navigation state machine issues. The memory says:
"The auto-next navigation state machine had two minor issues: no fallback path from NAVIGATING state for SPA route clicks, and missing .catch() in the Promise chain, though both were mitigated by existing safeguards."

Looking at auto-next.js:
1. The state machine has IDLE / NAVIGATING / LOCKED states.
2. In `onVideoEnded` (line 278), it sets state to NAVIGATING (line 286).
3. Then calls `probeQuizEntry().then(...)` which calls `resolveNext(cid).then(function(next) { ... }).catch(function (e) { swallow(e); ... })`.

Issue 1: Missing .catch() - Looking at line 290-306:
```js
probeQuizEntry().then(function (quiz) {
  if (quiz) {
    ...
    try { ... } catch (e) { resolveNext(cid).then(doNavigate); }
    return;
  }
  resolveNext(cid).then(function (next) {
    ...
  }).catch(function (e) {
    swallow(e);
    ...
  });
});
```

The outer `probeQuizEntry().then(...)` does NOT have a `.catch()`. If `probeQuizEntry()` rejects (which it shouldn't since it always resolves, but defensively...), it would be an unhandled rejection. But since `probeQuizEntry` always resolves (it creates a Promise that always calls `resolve`), this is a minor issue.

However, the inner `resolveNext(cid).then(...)` in the `if (quiz)` branch (line 295) does NOT have a `.catch()`:
```js
try { if (quiz.url) { window.location.href = quiz.url; } else if (quiz.el) { quiz.el.click(); } } catch (e) { resolveNext(cid).then(doNavigate); }
```
Here, `resolveNext(cid).then(doNavigate)` has no `.catch()`. If `resolveNext` rejects or `doNavigate` throws, it's an unhandled rejection.

Issue 2: No fallback from NAVIGATING state. When state is set to NAVIGATING (line 286), if the navigation fails or the SPA route click doesn't trigger a page load, the state stays NAVIGATING. Looking at `doNavigate`:
- For SPA route clicks (line 244): `setState(IDLE)` is called after click. 
- For URL navigation (line 240): `setTimeout(function () { try { window.location.href = url; } catch (e) { window.location.assign(url); } }, 300);` - but setState is NOT called. The state remains NAVIGATING.

Wait, actually for URL navigation, the page will reload, so the state doesn't matter. But if `window.location.href = url` fails silently (e.g., same-page navigation), the state stays NAVIGATING forever, blocking future ended events. But there's `navLockUntil` which expires after 4 seconds, so `isNavLocked()` would eventually return false. But the `state` variable itself stays NAVIGATING, and the only check is `if (state === LOCKED)` at line 279. So NAVIGATING state doesn't block future processing - only LOCKED does.

Actually wait, let me re-read. Line 279: `if (state === LOCKED) { ... return; }`. So if state is NAVIGATING, it doesn't return. It continues. So the NAVIGATING state doesn't actually block anything. The navLock (time-based) is what prevents duplicate triggers. So the state machine is a bit misleading - NAVIGATING doesn't really do anything special.

Hmm, but the issue mentioned is "no fallback path from NAVIGATING state for SPA route clicks". Looking at the SPA route handling (line 241-245):
```js
} else if (url.charAt(0) === '#' || /javascript:/.test(url)) {
  var link = $dom.one('a[href="' + url + '"]');
  if (link) { lockNav(); setState(IDLE); $log.info('click SPA next: ' + url); link.click(); }
  else { setState(LOCKED); toast('下一节为 SPA 路由但找不到可点击节点'); }
}
```
Here, `setState(IDLE)` is called after clicking. But if the SPA click fails to navigate (e.g., the link's click handler errors), the state goes back to IDLE, which means the next `ended` event would try again. That's actually a reasonable fallback.

But there's a subtle issue: if the SPA click succeeds but the video element fires `ended` again before the SPA navigation completes, `isNavLocked()` would block it (4 second lock). So that's covered.

The real issue is: after `doNavigate` for the SPA case, state is IDLE. If the click doesn't actually navigate (SPA route change fails silently), the user is stuck on the same page with state IDLE. The next `ended` event would try again, but the same video is already in `seenVideos`/`handledUrls`, so it would be skipped (line 282-283). So the auto-next would be stuck - it won't retry the same video. This is a potential issue but it's by design (avoid infinite loops).

Let me now look at the Promise chain issue more carefully. The `.catch()` at line 302-305 calls `swallow(e)`. As I noted, `swallow` may not be defined in auto-next.js's IIFE scope. This is the key issue.

Now let me also look at performance issues:

1. tamper-guard.js line 106-107:
```js
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () { setInterval(guardTick, 2000); guardTick(); });
if (document.readyState !== 'loading') { setInterval(guardTick, 2000); guardTick(); }
```
If `document.readyState !== 'loading'` (which is common), BOTH the DOMContentLoaded listener AND the immediate setInterval will fire. This means `setInterval(guardTick, 2000)` is called TWICE, resulting in guardTick running every 1 second effectively (two intervals). This is a bug - double interval registration.

2. In popup-quiz.js, the `_pqHandled` object grows unbounded - every handled popup adds a fingerprint key that's never cleaned up. Over a long session, this could grow large. Same for `_quizHandled` in quiz.js.

3. In auto-next.js, `findNextDOM()` calls `$dom.all('a')` and `$dom.all('button, [role="button"], [onclick], span, li, div')` which could be very expensive on large pages (querying ALL spans and divs).

4. In keyboard-shortcuts.js, `activeVideo()` calls `document.querySelectorAll('video')` on every keydown, which is fine.

5. In tamper-guard.js, `guardTick` calls `document.querySelectorAll('video')` every 2 seconds, which is fine.

Now let me look at readability/maintainability issues:

1. Site files duplication: zhihuishu.js, icourse163.js, xuetangx.js, icve.js all follow the exact same pattern:
```js
var SITE = { dialogSels: [...], optionSels: [...], answerBtnSels: [...] };
function siteTickQuestions() {
  if (detectSite() !== 'site') return 0;
  try { return popupQuizTick(SITE); } catch (e) { swallow(e); return 0; }
}
try { window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.siteTickQuestions = siteTickQuestions; } catch (e) {}
```
And renwei.js, unipus.js, ucampus.js, ilabx.js, chaoxing-exam.js follow a similar pattern with `quizTick` instead of `popupQuizTick`.

These could be refactored into a factory function.

2. The `detectSite()` call outside try/catch in all site files - if detectSite throws, the tick function throws. This is a runtime exception risk.

3. Inconsistent `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY` access: some files use `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.xxx` directly (popup-quiz.js line 20, 103), while others use `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY = window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY || {}` first (zhihuishu-exam.js line 339). The former pattern risks TypeError if `__CX_FORCE_PLAY` is undefined.

OK let me also check the tamper-guard.js double interval issue more carefully:

```js
function init() {
  try {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () { setInterval(guardTick, 2000); guardTick(); });
    if (document.readyState !== 'loading') { setInterval(guardTick, 2000); guardTick(); }
  } catch (e) {}
  ...
}
```

If `document.readyState` is 'interactive' or 'complete' (which is common since the script loads with `setTimeout(bootstrap, 300)` or similar delay), then:
1. The `DOMContentLoaded` listener is added (but won't fire since DOM already loaded)
2. `document.readyState !== 'loading'` is true, so `setInterval(guardTick, 2000)` is called immediately

Wait, actually if readyState is 'interactive', DOMContentLoaded hasn't fired yet but will soon. So:
1. DOMContentLoaded listener is added
2. readyState !== 'loading' is true (it's 'interactive'), so setInterval is called immediately
3. Later, DOMContentLoaded fires, and setInterval is called AGAIN

So we get TWO intervals. This is a real bug. It should be:
```js
if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () { setInterval(guardTick, 2000); guardTick(); });
} else {
  setInterval(guardTick, 2000); guardTick();
}
```

Now let me also check the video-ended-notify.js `TOP` variable:
```js

```
In strict mode inside an IIFE, `this` is `undefined`, so `TOP = undefined || window = window`. This is fine. But if not in strict mode, `this` would be `window` in a normal function. Since the IIFE has `'use strict'`, `this` is `undefined`, so `TOP` is `window`. OK, this is fine.

But wait, `var TOP = this || window;` is at line 12, and `var ns = window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY;` is at line 13. These are defined AFTER the `swallow` variable at line 8. But the idempotency guard is at line 36-37. So if the script is injected twice, the second injection would define `TK`, `swallow`, `TOP`, `ns` again before hitting the guard. This is harmless.

Wait, but the user listed it as a problem to find. Let me re-read... Actually the code at line 28 DOES handle the string case. But wait, maybe the issue is different. Let me look more carefully.

Actually, looking again at url.js:
```js
function urlParam(href, names) {
    if (typeof names === 'string') names = [names];   // 兼容单键名字符串入参（避免按字符遍历）
    for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
      const m = href.match(new RegExp('[?&#]' + names[i] + '=([^&#]+)', 'i'));
```

This handles the string case. But what if `href` is not a string? If href is undefined/null, `href.match` would throw. There's no type guard on href. Also, `names[i]` is interpolated into a RegExp without escaping - if the key name contains regex special characters (like `.`, `+`, `(`, etc.), it would break or match unintended patterns. For example, a key name like `course.id` would match `courseXid` due to the `.`. This is a regex injection / correctness issue.

Also, the JSDoc says `@param {string} href` but there's no runtime guard. If href is undefined (e.g., from a failed topHref), it throws TypeError.

Now let me check the `lsGet`/`lsSet` vs JSDoc contract issue mentioned by the user. Looking at utils.js:
- `lsGet` (line 173): JSDoc says "读取 localStorage（原始字符串；缺失或异常回退默认值 d）" and "@returns {string|*} 命中时返回原始字符串，否则返回默认 d". The implementation returns `v == null ? d : v`. This matches the JSDoc.
- `lsSet` (line 182): JSDoc says "写入 localStorage（原始值经 String(v) 转字符串；异常被 swallow 静默返回 false）". But the implementation: `try { window.localStorage.setItem(k, String(v)); return true; } catch (e) { return false; }`. The JSDoc says "异常被 swallow 静默返回 false" but the implementation does NOT call `swallow(e)` - it just returns false! So the JSDoc contract says swallow is called (which would record the error in the ring buffer), but the implementation silently returns false without recording. This is the "lsGet/lsSet 实现与 JSDoc 契约不符" issue.

Wait, let me re-read the JSDoc for lsSet: "异常被 swallow 静默返回 false". Hmm, this could be read as "异常被 swallow，静默返回 false" (exception is swallowed, silently returns false) OR "异常被 swallow 静默，返回 false". The Chinese is ambiguous. But comparing to lsGet which says "缺失或异常回退默认值 d" and doesn't mention swallow, while lsSet explicitly mentions swallow... Actually looking at the lsFlag function (line 197): `try { ... } catch (e) { return false; }` - also doesn't swallow. And `_lsRaw` (line 191) DOES call swallow. So there's inconsistency: some LS wrappers call swallow, others don't.

Actually, the more clear issue: `lsSet` JSDoc says "异常被 swallow 静默返回 false" implying swallow is called, but the code `catch (e) { return false; }` does NOT call swallow. This means errors from lsSet are NOT recorded in the error ring buffer, making them invisible to diagnostics. This is a real contract mismatch.

Similarly `lsGet` JSDoc doesn't mention swallow, and indeed it doesn't swallow - it just returns d. So lsGet is consistent with its JSDoc but inconsistent with the observability pattern (other LS helpers like _lsRaw do swallow).

```js
var on = (_qa = (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') ? localStorage.getItem('cx_quiz_auto') : null, _qa !== '0');   // 与 controls 默认开一致
```

这里使用了 `_qa` 变量在它声明之前！由于 `var _qa` 声明被提升（hoisting），它不会报 ReferenceError，但在赋值语句执行时 `_qa` 是 undefined。让我仔细看：

- `var _qa;` 在下一行声明。

由于 var 提升，`_qa` 在函数作用域内是可见的，所以这个不会报错。但是这是一个非常糟糕的写法——在声明前使用变量，可读性极差，容易引起误解。

另一个问题：`renderVideoList` 在每次刷新都用 `wrap.innerHTML = html`，这是高频热路径（节流到 150ms）。而且每次都重建整个 DOM，性能不佳。但这是已有节流的。

另外注意 `_refreshQuizZone` 中第 96 行的判断：
```js
if (!st || !st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0) {
```
这个条件判断运算符优先级问题：`&&` 优先级高于 `||`，所以这等价于 `(!st) || (!st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0)`。这看起来是正确的，但逻辑上有点奇怪：如果 `!st.scanned` 为 true 且 `st.scanned !== 0` 为 true，则进入。也就是 `st.scanned` 是 falsy 但不是 0（比如 undefined/null/NaN/''）。这应该是想处理"未扫描"的情况。逻辑是对的，但可读性差，应该加括号。

11. In metrics.js, `_errBuf` and `_errCount` are defined here but used in utils.js's `swallow`. The comment in utils.js (lines 8-9) acknowledges this forward reference. The risk: if `swallow` is called during module evaluation (before metrics.js executes), `_errBuf` is undefined (hoisted, not yet assigned `[]`), and `_errBuf.push(...)` would throw TypeError. The `swallow` function itself has a try/catch around the buffer write (lines 26-31), so the TypeError would be caught by the outer... wait, no. Let me re-read swallow:

```js
function swallow(e, tag) {
    _errCount++;            // line 25 - if _errCount is undefined, this is NaN (no throw)
    try {
      var _entry = { ... };
      if (e && e.stack) _entry.stack = e.stack;
      _errBuf.push(_entry);   // line 29 - if _errBuf is undefined, TypeError!
      if (_errBuf.length > 50) _errBuf.shift();
    } catch (_) {}           // line 31 - catches the TypeError, continues
    if (!DEBUG) return;       // line 33 - DEBUG might be undefined too if config.js hasn't loaded
    try { console.warn(...); } catch (_) {}
  }
```

So if `swallow` is called before metrics.js evaluates:
- Line 25: `_errCount++` → `undefined++` → NaN (no throw in non-strict, but in strict mode? Actually `undefined++` in strict mode... let me think. `var x; x++;` is valid even in strict mode, x becomes NaN. But `_errCount` is hoisted via var, so it's declared. `undefined++` → NaN. No throw.)
- Line 29: `_errBuf.push(...)` → `_errBuf` is undefined → TypeError
- Line 31: `catch (_) {}` catches it, continues
- Line 33: `if (!DEBUG)` - DEBUG is hoisted (var in config.js), undefined → `!undefined` → true → return

So `swallow` would silently fail to record the error but NOT throw. The error is lost. This is the "swallow 跨 IIFE 未定义" risk. It's mitigated by the try/catch, but the error telemetry is lost for any swallow call before metrics.js loads. The comment acknowledges this but it's a real latent issue.

Also, `dbg` has the same issue: line 15 `if (!DEBUG) return;` - if called before config.js loads, DEBUG is undefined → returns. Safe. But line 16 `try { console.log... } catch (e) { swallow(e); }` - if DEBUG is truthy and console.log throws, swallow is called, which might fail as above. Edge case.

12. In utils.js, the `toast` function (line 215-231) has a bare `catch (e) {}` at line 230 - it doesn't call swallow. So toast errors are silently dropped without telemetry. Inconsistent with the observability pattern.

13. In url.js, `urlParam` (line 27-34): 
- No guard on `href` being a string. If `href` is undefined (e.g., topHref returns '' on cross-origin), `href.match` throws TypeError. But topHref returns '' (empty string) on error, not undefined. `''.match(...)` returns null. So `if (m)` is false, returns null. OK for empty string. But if someone passes undefined directly, it throws.
- `new RegExp('[?&#]' + names[i] + '=([^&#]+)', 'i')` - `names[i]` is not regex-escaped. If a key name contains regex metacharacters (`.`, `+`, `?`, etc.), the regex would be incorrect. For example, `urlParam(href, 'course.id')` would match `courseXid` because `.` matches any char. This is a correctness bug for keys with special characters.
- The JSDoc says `@param {string|string[]} names` and line 28 handles string→array. But there's no guard if `names` is undefined/null. `names.length` would throw.

14. Let me check the `lsSet` JSDoc contract mismatch more carefully. utils.js line 176-184:
```js
  /**
   * 写入 localStorage（原始值经 String(v) 转字符串；异常被 swallow 静默返回 false）。
   * 注意：不做 JSON.stringify，对象会成 "[object Object]"（如需结构化存储请调用方自行 JSON.stringify）。
   * @param {string} k - 键名
   * @param {*} v - 待写入值
   */
  function lsSet(k, v) {
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(k, String(v)); return true; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
```

The JSDoc says "异常被 swallow 静默返回 false" - this states that on exception, swallow is called AND returns false. But the implementation `catch (e) { return false; }` does NOT call swallow. This is a clear contract mismatch. The error is not recorded in the telemetry ring buffer.

Compare with `_lsSetRaw` (line 192): `function _lsSetRaw(k, v) { try { ... } catch (e) { swallow(e, 'ls'); return false; } }` - this one DOES swallow. So there's inconsistency between `lsSet` (no swallow) and `_lsSetRaw` (swallow). 

Similarly, `lsFlag` (line 194-196): `catch (e) { return !!def; }` - no swallow.
`lsSetFlag` (line 197-199): `catch (e) { return false; }` - no swallow.
`lsRemove` (line 208): `catch (e) { return false; }` - no swallow.

So most LS wrappers don't swallow, while `_lsRaw` and `_lsSetRaw` do. And the JSDoc for `lsSet` explicitly claims swallow is called. This is the contract mismatch.

15. Performance: In main-loop.js `_loopTick`, every tick (every 2s by default) calls:
- `refreshTargets()` 
- `scanVideos(document)` + `neutralizeGlobalPause(window)` - full DOM scan
- `probePauseNeutralized()` + `probeRateNeutralized()` - these call `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor` each time
- `installPrototypePauseNeutralize()` - re-installs prototype pause EVERY tick (F-B4 design)
- `applyUserRateAll()` - walks ALL videos and iframes every tick
- Various site-specific tick functions
- `Store.emit('videos:scanned')` if panel visible

The `scanVideos` → `walkVideos` does `root.querySelectorAll('*')` on shadow hosts (line 275) and `root.querySelectorAll(_containerSel)` + `_ct.getElementsByTagName('*')` (lines 280-284). This is a FULL DOM traversal every 2 seconds, including `querySelectorAll('*')` which is expensive. Plus `applyUserRateAll` does another full `getElementsByTagName('video')` + iframe walk. This is a significant performance concern for large pages.

Also `installPrototypePauseNeutralize` reassigns `HTMLMediaElement.prototype.pause` every 2s. While this is documented as F-B4 design (counter anti-tampering), it's a performance cost and also means any platform code checking `pause.toString()` between ticks would see different results (in polite mode).

16. In config.js, `applyUserRateAll` (line 244-263) uses `root.getElementsByTagName('video')` and recursively walks iframes via `root.getElementsByTagName('iframe')`. This is a SEPARATE full traversal from `scanVideos`/`walkVideos`. So each tick does at least 2 full DOM traversals (scanVideos + applyUserRateAll). These could be merged.

But walkVideos is recursive (calls itself for iframes), so `siteTaskContainerSel()` is called once per recursion level. For a page with many iframes, that's many `detectSite()` calls. The comment says "评审#6：缓存容器选择器，避免递归每层重复拼接与 detectSite 调用" but the caching is only within one walkVideos frame (line 264), not across recursive calls. Each recursive call to walkVideos re-calls siteTaskContainerSel(). So the "cache" doesn't actually prevent repeated detectSite calls across recursion. This is a performance issue.

Hmm wait, let me re-read. Line 264-268:

This is at the top of walkVideos. Each recursive call to walkVideos executes this. So yes, each recursion level calls siteTaskContainerSel() → detectSite(). The comment claims this is caching but it's only caching within a single invocation, not across recursive invocations. For a page with N iframes at depth D, detectSite is called O(N*D) times per scan, when it could be cached once globally (since the site doesn't change mid-page).

20. One more: in storage.js, `KVS.set` in indexeddb mode (line 50-54):
```js
      _ready.then(function (ok) {
        if (!ok) { lsSet(k, val); return; }
        try { var tx = idb.transaction('kv', 'readwrite'); tx.objectStore('kv').put(val, k); }
        catch (e) { lsSet(k, val); }
      });
```
There's no `onerror`/`oncomplete` handler on the transaction. If the IDB write fails asynchronously (after the try/catch), the error is silently lost and there's no fallback to localStorage. The `catch (e)` only catches synchronous errors during transaction creation, not async IDB errors. Also, the function returns undefined (no return of the promise), so callers can't await or handle failures.

Now let me also check the `throttle` function for a potential issue. Line 77-94:
```js
  function throttle(fn, wait) {
    var last = 0, timer = null, ctxA = null, argsA = null;
    return function () {
      var now = Date.now(), ctx = this, args = arguments;
      var remaining = wait - (now - last);
      if (remaining <= 0) {
        if (timer) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; }
        last = now;
        safeCall(function () { fn.apply(ctx, args); }, 'throttle');
      } else if (!timer) {
        ctxA = ctx; argsA = args;
        timer = setTimeout(function () {
          last = Date.now(); timer = null;
          safeCall(function () { fn.apply(ctxA, argsA); }, 'throttle');
        }, remaining);
      }
    };
  }
```
Issue: when `remaining > 0` AND `timer` already exists, the call is dropped (neither executed nor queued). This means the trailing call uses the FIRST call's ctx/args within the window, not the LAST. Standard throttle should use the last call's args for the trailing edge. Here, `ctxA`/`argsA` are only set when `!timer` (first call in window). Subsequent calls within the window don't update `ctxA`/`argsA`. So the trailing call uses stale (first) arguments. This is a behavior bug for throttled functions that depend on the latest arguments (e.g., a search input where the user typed more text).

This is a real logic bug in throttle.

Actually, I realize I should double-check one thing: the `h()` function event detection. Line 249-253:
```js
else if (k.length > 2 && k.indexOf('on') === 0 && typeof v === 'function') {
  var _evt = k.slice(2).toLowerCase();
  if (('on' + _evt) in el) el.addEventListener(_evt, v);
  else el.setAttribute(k, v);
}
```
The issue: `('on' + _evt) in el` - for a div, `'onclick' in el` is true (inherited from HTMLElement). But `'oninput' in el` is also true. However, for custom elements or SVG elements, some 'on*' properties may not exist. The guard is reasonable. But there's a subtle issue: `k.indexOf('on') === 0` matches any key starting with 'on' - like 'online', 'ontoggle', 'onvolumechange' which are valid events, but also 'ontology', 'onset' which are not. The `('on' + _evt) in el` check filters these. For 'ontology' → _evt = 'tology' → 'ontology' in el → false → setAttribute. OK, this works. Not a bug.

But there's another issue: if `v` is a function and the key starts with 'on' but `('on' + _evt) in el` is false, it does `el.setAttribute(k, v)` - setting an attribute to a function. `setAttribute` calls `toString()` on the function, which produces the function source code as a string attribute. This is probably not intended. But it's an edge case.

Actually, let me reconsider the throttle issue. Let me re-read:

```js
  function throttle(fn, wait) {
    var last = 0, timer = null, ctxA = null, argsA = null;
    return function () {
      var now = Date.now(), ctx = this, args = arguments;
      var remaining = wait - (now - last);
      if (remaining <= 0) {
        if (timer) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; }
        last = now;
        safeCall(function () { fn.apply(ctx, args); }, 'throttle');
      } else if (!timer) {
        ctxA = ctx; argsA = args;
        timer = setTimeout(function () {
          last = Date.now(); timer = null;
          safeCall(function () { fn.apply(ctxA, argsA); }, 'throttle');
        }, remaining);
      }
    };
  }
```

The issue is in the `else if (!timer)` branch. When `remaining > 0` and `timer` is already set (i.e., a trailing call is already scheduled), the new call's `ctx` and `args` are NOT captured. The trailing call will use the `ctxA`/`argsA` from the FIRST call that set the timer, not the LATEST call. 

Standard throttle behavior: the trailing edge should fire with the LATEST arguments. Here it fires with the first arguments in the window. This is a real bug.

Additionally, calls that arrive when `timer` is already set are completely dropped (not even updating argsA). This means if fn depends on the latest arguments (e.g., a resize handler that needs the latest dimensions, or a search that needs the latest query), it will process stale data.

Actually, I want to verify one more potential issue. In state.js, the `emit` function for cross-frame toast forwarding (lines 34-36):
```js
      if (ev === 'ui:toast' && typeof window !== 'undefined' && window !== window.top) {
        try { window.top.postMessage({ __cxFeedToast: Array.from(arguments).slice(1) }, '*'); } catch (e) { swallow(e, 'store.emit.fwd'); }
      }
```
This forwards to `window.top` with `'*'` as targetOrigin. Using `'*'` means the message is sent to any origin. This is a security concern - any page can receive this message if it's the top frame. The message only contains toast text, so the impact is low, but it's a minor security smell. The receiver (lines 56-65) checks for `__cxFeedToast` property but doesn't verify the origin. A malicious top frame could... well, it's the top frame, so it already has full control. Not a real issue.

But the receiver (lines 58-63) doesn't check `e.origin`. Any cross-origin frame could send a `__cxFeedToast` message and trigger `emit('ui:toast', ...)`. This could be used to inject arbitrary toast messages. Low severity but worth noting.

## 一、utils/utils.js

### 问题 1:`swallow` 跨 IIFE 前向引用 `_errBuf`/`_errCount` —— 运行时异常(遥测丢失)

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\foundation\utils\utils.js` 第 25、27、29、30 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  function swallow(e, tag) {
    _errCount++;                    // 第25行:引用 metrics.js 的 var
    try {
      var _entry = { ... };
      _errBuf.push(_entry);         // 第29行:引用 metrics.js 的 var
      if (_errBuf.length > 50) _errBuf.shift();
    } catch (_) {}
  ```
- **具体表现**:`_errBuf` 和 `_errCount` 实际定义在 `state/metrics.js` 第 33-34 行。由于同处一个 IIFE 闭包,`var` 声明会被提升(hoist),但赋值 `[]`/`0` 要等到 metrics.js 文本求值时才发生。utils.js 是第一个模块、metrics.js 是第三个。如果在 metrics.js 求值之前有任何代码路径调用 `swallow`(例如第二个模块在顶层求值时抛错并被捕获),`_errBuf` 此时为 `undefined`,`_errBuf.push(...)` 抛 TypeError,被第 31 行 `catch (_) {}` 静默吞掉。
- **后果**:启动早期阶段的错误遥测完全丢失,黑匣子环形缓冲无记录,诊断时无法追溯启动期失败。文件顶部注释(第 4-9 行)已意识到此风险并警告"勿在本文件顶层即时调用 dbg()",但未对 `swallow` 做同等防御。
- **改进建议**:在 `swallow` 内部对 `_errBuf`/`_errCount` 做 `typeof` 守卫,或将 `_errBuf`/`_errCount` 的声明前移到 utils.js(与 swallow 同文件),metrics.js 仅保留读取函数。

### 问题 2:`lsSet` 实现与 JSDoc 契约不符 —— 可观测性缺陷

- **文件**:同上,第 176-184 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  /**
   * 写入 localStorage（原始值经 String(v) 转字符串；异常被 swallow 静默返回 false）。
   */
  function lsSet(k, v) {
    try { window.localStorage.setItem(k, String(v)); return true; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  ```
- **具体表现**:JSDoc 明确声称"异常被 swallow 静默返回 false",但 `catch` 分支仅 `return false`,**未调用 `swallow(e)`**。对比同文件第 192 行 `_lsSetRaw` 则正确调用了 `swallow(e, 'ls')`。同类问题还出现在 `lsFlag`(第 196 行)、`lsSetFlag`(第 199 行)、`lsRemove`(第 208 行)——均未 swallow。
- **后果**:`lsSet` 等函数的 localStorage 写入失败(如配额超限、隐私模式禁用)不会进入错误环形缓冲,诊断面板和黑匣子完全看不到这些失败,排查"设置刷新后丢失"类问题时误判为"未触发保存"而非"保存失败"。
- **改进建议**:统一所有 localStorage 包装器的异常处理——要么全部 swallow(与 `_lsRaw`/`_lsSetRaw` 一致),要么修正 JSDoc 去掉"被 swallow"的描述。推荐前者以保持可观测性一致。

### 问题 3:`throttle` 尾沿使用陈旧参数 —— 逻辑错误

- **文件**:同上,第 77-94 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  function throttle(fn, wait) {
    var last = 0, timer = null, ctxA = null, argsA = null;
    return function () {
      var now = Date.now(), ctx = this, args = arguments;
      var remaining = wait - (now - last);
      if (remaining <= 0) { ... }
      else if (!timer) {           // 仅在无 timer 时捕获
        ctxA = ctx; argsA = args;  // 后续调用不更新 ctxA/argsA
        timer = setTimeout(function () { ... fn.apply(ctxA, argsA); ... }, remaining);
      }
      // timer 已存在时:新调用的 ctx/args 被完全丢弃
    };
  }
  ```
- **具体表现**:当 `remaining > 0` 且 `timer` 已存在时(即节流窗口内第二次及以后的调用),新调用的 `ctx`/`args` 既不执行也不更新到 `ctxA`/`argsA`。尾沿回调使用的是**窗口内第一次调用**的参数,而非最后一次。
- **后果**:标准节流语义是"尾沿触发最近一次调用"。此处尾沿触发的是最早一次。对于依赖最新参数的场景(如面板刷新、搜索输入、resize 回调),会处理陈旧数据导致 UI 与实际状态不一致。
- **改进建议**:将 `else if (!timer)` 改为 `else { ctxA = ctx; argsA = args; if (!timer) { timer = setTimeout(...); } }`——无论 timer 是否存在都更新最新参数,仅在没有待执行 timer 时才新建。

### 问题 4:`toast` 裸 catch 未记录遥测 —— 可观测性缺口

- **文件**:同上,第 230 行
- **问题代码**:`} catch (e) {}`
- **具体表现**:`toast` 函数整体被 `try/catch` 包裹,但 catch 块为空,既不 swallow 也不返回错误信息。
- **后果**:toast 渲染失败(如 `document.body` 不存在、`requestAnimationFrame` 不可用)完全静默,无任何遥测记录,与全脚本"swallow 兜底"的可观测性约定不一致。
- **改进建议**:改为 `catch (e) { swallow(e, 'toast'); }`。

### 问题 5:`debounce` 与 `throttle` 风格不一致 —— 可维护性(轻微)

- **文件**:同上,第 102-104 行
- **问题代码**:`debounce` 使用 `let`/`const`,而 `throttle`(第 78 行)使用 `var`。
- **后果**:同一文件内变量声明风格不统一,增加阅读认知负担。在严格模式下功能无差异。
- **改进建议**:统一为 `var`(与文件其余部分一致)或统一为 `let`/`const`。

## 二、utils/url.js

### 问题 6:`urlParam` 未转义 RegExp 元字符 —— 逻辑错误(正确性)

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\foundation\utils\url.js` 第 30 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  const m = href.match(new RegExp('[?&#]' + names[i] + '=([^&#]+)', 'i'));
  ```
- **具体表现**:`names[i]` 直接拼入正则表达式,未做元字符转义。若键名含 `.`、`+`、`(`、`?` 等(如 `course.id`、`chapter.no`),`.` 会匹配任意字符,导致误匹配。例如 `urlParam(href, 'course.id')` 会匹配 `courseXid=...`。
- **后果**:参数解析返回错误值,定向白名单/桥客户端可能取到非预期数据,引发续播目标错乱。
- **改进建议**:对 `names[i]` 做 RegExp 转义,如 `names[i].replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')` 后再拼接。

### 问题 7:`urlParam` 未对 `href` 做类型守卫 —— 潜在运行时异常

- **文件**:同上,第 29 行
- **问题代码**:`for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) { const m = href.match(...`
- **具体表现**:JSDoc 标注 `@param {string} href`,但无运行时类型守卫。若调用方传入 `undefined`(如 `topHref()` 在极端跨域场景返回空串尚可,但调用方直接传 `undefined` 时),`href.match` 抛 `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined`。同时 `names` 为 `null`/`undefined` 时 `names.length` 也会抛错。第 28 行 `if (typeof names === 'string') names = [names];` 已处理字符串入参(这一点已修复),但未处理 `null`/`undefined`。
- **后果**:调用链上游传入非法类型时直接抛异常,若未被外层 try/catch 包裹则中断调用方逻辑。
- **改进建议**:在函数入口添加 `if (!href || typeof href !== 'string') return null;` 和 `if (!names) return null;`。

## 三、state/state.js

### 问题 8:`Store.state._loopTimer` 镜像在定时器重启后失同步 —— 逻辑错误(已文档化但未修复)

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\foundation\state\state.js` 第 6-8 行(注释说明)与 `bootstrap/main-loop.js` 第 124 行(镜像赋值)
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  // main-loop.js 第124行:仅此处镜像一次
  Store.state._loopTimer = _loopTimer;
  ```
- **具体表现**:`_loopTimer` 在 `panel-core.js:187` 和 `commands.js:79` 被重新赋值(`clearInterval` 后 `setInterval` 重启),但这两处均未同步更新 `Store.state._loopTimer`。state.js 顶部注释(第 7-8 行)已明确承认此问题:"对基本类型(如 _loopTimer 重启后的新 id)镜像不会自动同步,需在赋值处一并更新镜像"。
- **后果**:用户在面板调整 `RESCAN_INTERVAL` 后,`Store.state._loopTimer` 仍指向已清除的旧定时器 id。诊断面板/黑匣子读取该镜像会误判"循环仍在以旧间隔运行",而实际旧定时器已被 clearInterval,新定时器 id 未反映在镜像中。
- **改进建议**:在 `panel-core.js:187` 和 `commands.js:79` 的 `_loopTimer = setInterval(...)` 赋值后,追加 `Store.state._loopTimer = _loopTimer;`。或将 `_loopTimer` 改为通过 `Store.set('_loopTimer', v)` 写入以自动同步(需配合状态收敛迁移)。

### 问题 9:跨帧 toast postMessage 未校验来源 —— 安全/可维护性(轻微)

- **文件**:同上,第 56-65 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  window.addEventListener('message', function (e) {
    try {
      var d = e && e.data;
      if (d && d.__cxFeedToast && Array.isArray(d.__cxFeedToast)) emit.apply(null, ['ui:toast'].concat(d.__cxFeedToast));
    } catch (err) { swallow(err, 'store.feed.rcv'); }
  });
  ```
- **具体表现**:接收子帧转发的 toast 消息时,未校验 `e.origin`。任何嵌入的跨域 iframe 都可以构造 `{ __cxFeedToast: [...] }` 消息向顶层帧注入任意 toast 文本。
- **后果**:恶意/被篡改的嵌入页可向用户面板注入误导性提示文本。影响较低(仅 UI 提示),但违背最小信任原则。
- **改进建议**:校验 `e.source` 的归属域,或维护一个可信子帧 origin 白名单。

## 四、state/metrics.js

### 问题 10:`_errBuf`/`_errCount` 归属与 swallow 的跨模块耦合 —— 可维护性/潜在运行时异常

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\foundation\state\metrics.js` 第 33-34 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  var _errBuf = [];          // 被 utils.js swallow 直接读写
  var _errCount = 0;         // 被 utils.js swallow 直接自增
  ```
- **具体表现**:这两个变量的唯一写入方是 `utils.js` 的 `swallow`(第 25-30 行),但声明在 `metrics.js`。注释(第 30-32 行)解释了"前向引用"的安全性依赖 IIFE 内 var 提升 + 运行时调用。这是问题 1 的根因——变量声明与使用分离,使得"在 metrics.js 求值前调用 swallow"成为不安全路径。
- **后果**:见问题 1。此外,模块边界不清晰:修改 metrics.js 时不易意识到 utils.js 直接依赖这两个 var 的初始化时序。
- **改进建议**:将 `_errBuf`/`_errCount` 声明移至 utils.js(与 `swallow` 同文件),metrics.js 仅保留 `recentErrors`/`errorCount` 读取函数(通过闭包引用同文件变量)。

## 五、bootstrap/core.js

### 问题 11:`globalErrorHandler` 未接入遥测 —— 可观测性缺口

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\bootstrap\core.js` 第 15-17 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  function globalErrorHandler(ev) {
    try { console.error('[CX-FORCE] 运行时错误:', ev.message, (ev.error && ev.error.stack) ? ev.error.stack : ''); } catch (e) {}
  }
  ```
- **具体表现**:全局错误处理器仅 `console.error`,未调用 `swallow(ev.error, 'globalError')`。未被 try/catch 捕获的运行时错误(如异步 Promise rejection 回调中的异常)不会进入错误环形缓冲。
- **后果**:诊断面板/黑匣子看不到全局未捕获错误,与 swallow 吞掉的错误形成遥测盲区。排查"脚本整体不执行"时,控制台可见但黑匣子无记录。
- **改进建议**:在 `console.error` 后追加 `try { swallow(ev.error || ev.message, 'globalError'); } catch (_) {}`。

### 问题 12:core.js 未关闭 IIFE —— 结构依赖(非独立可运行)

- **文件**:同上,第 1-22 行
- **具体表现**:core.js 以 `(function () { 'use strict';` 开头(第 2 行),但文件末尾(第 22 行)没有对应的 `})();` 闭合。这表明 core.js 依赖构建拼接——后续文件(如 main-loop.js、各 foundation 模块)的代码被拼接到同一 IIFE 内部,最终由某个文件闭合。
- **后果**:core.js 无法独立运行/测试;新增开发者单独打开此文件会看到"未闭合的 IIFE"。这是拼接式架构的固有特征,非 bug,但影响可维护性。
- **改进建议**:在文件头部注释中标注"本文件为 IIFE 片段,需与其他模块拼接后运行,闭合括号在 xxx.js"。已有的注释已部分说明,可补充 IIFE 边界信息。

## 六、dom/lifecycle.js

### 问题 13:`_attachStore` 引用缺少 typeof 守卫 —— 防御风格不一致

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\dom\lifecycle.js` 第 75 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  try { Object.defineProperty(window, _ak, { ..., value: _attachStore ? _attachStore.value : undefined }); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
  ```
- **具体表现**:`_attachStore` 定义在 `engine/targeting.js:51`(`var _attachStore = null;`)。lifecycle.js 直接引用 `_attachStore` 而非用 `typeof _attachStore !== 'undefined'` 守卫。对比同文件第 65 行 `if (typeof restorePrototypePause === 'function')` 和第 138 行 `if (typeof Store !== 'undefined' && Store.clearSubs)` 均做了 typeof 守卫。
- **后果**:由于同 IIFE 内 var 提升,`_attachStore` 始终已声明(hoist 为 undefined),不会抛 ReferenceError,实际安全。但防御风格不一致——若未来重构将 targeting.js 移出此 IIFE(如改为独立模块),此处会直接 ReferenceError,而其他有守卫的引用不会。
- **改进建议**:统一为 `value: (typeof _attachStore !== 'undefined' && _attachStore) ? _attachStore.value : undefined`,或至少在注释中标注跨模块依赖来源(已有注释说明,可接受)。

### 问题 14:`clearSubs('__ev:ui:toast')` 保留订阅但 DOM 已移除 —— 逻辑不一致(轻微)

- **文件**:同上,第 138 行
- **问题代码**:`try { if (typeof Store !== 'undefined' && Store.clearSubs) Store.clearSubs('__ev:ui:toast'); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }`
- **具体表现**:卸载时保留 `__ev:ui:toast` 事件订阅(注释说"避免顶层帧接收子帧提示能力丢失"),但紧接着第 ⑩ 步(第 123-132 行)已移除 Toast DOM 节点(`__cxToast`),第 ⑪ 步(第 139 行)删除了 `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY` 命名空间。保留的订阅者函数仍会引用已移除的 DOM 节点。
- **后果**:卸载后若子帧仍通过 postMessage 转发 toast,顶层 `emit('ui:toast')` 会调用这些"孤儿订阅者",它们尝试操作已移除的 DOM 抛错,被 swallow 吞掉。功能上无害(已卸载),但逻辑上矛盾——保留了一个无法正常渲染的订阅路径。
- **改进建议**:若卸载后确实不需要 toast 能力( DOM 已移除),应 `clearSubs()` 清空全部订阅而非保留 `__ev:ui:toast`。若需保留跨帧能力,则不应在第 ⑩ 步移除 Toast DOM。

## 七、meta-config/config.js

### 问题 15:`applyUserRateAll` 独立全量遍历,与 `scanVideos` 重复 —— 性能瓶颈

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\foundation\meta-config\config.js` 第 244-263 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  function applyUserRateAll() {
    ...
    function walk(root) {
      var vs = root.getElementsByTagName('video');
      ...
      var fs = root.getElementsByTagName('iframe');
      for (var j = 0; j < fs.length; j++) { try { if (fs[j].contentDocument) walk(fs[j].contentDocument); } catch (e) { swallow(e); } }
    }
    walk(document);
  }
  ```
- **具体表现**:每轮主循环(main-loop.js 第 69 行)调用 `applyUserRateAll()`,它独立执行一次全量 `getElementsByTagName('video')` + 递归 iframe 下钻。而同一轮的 `scanVideos(document)`(第 40 行)已经通过 `walkVideos` 遍历了所有视频和 iframe。两次全量 DOM 遍历存在显著重叠。
- **后果**:每 2s 一次的主循环中,DOM 被完整遍历两次。对于含大量视频/iframe 的页面(如多标签课程页),主线程开销翻倍,可能导致卡顿。
- **改进建议**:将倍速施加逻辑合并到 `scanVideos`→`walkVideos` 的 `onVideo` 回调中(在 `overrideVideo` 内一并处理 `playbackRate`),消除独立的第二次遍历。

### 问题 16:每轮重装原型 `pause` 的性能与检测副作用 —— 性能(已文档化为 F-B4 设计)

- **文件**:同上,第 96-114 行(`installPrototypePauseNeutralize`)与 `main-loop.js` 第 56 行
- **问题代码**:main-loop.js 第 56 行:`try { if (usePrototypeNeutralize()) installPrototypePauseNeutralize(); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }`
- **具体表现**:每 2s 重新赋值 `HTMLMediaElement.prototype.pause`。注释(main-loop.js 第 41 行)明确这是 F-B4 刻意设计(对抗平台 `use strict` 下描述符还原)。但每次重装都创建新的函数对象(`protoPauseNeutral`/`protoPauseLiteral`),并触发原型属性变更。
- **后果**:每 2s 一次原型属性写入 + 新函数对象分配。在 `POLITE_MODE` 下,重装后 `pause.toString()` 内容会变化(虽然字面量已规避),平台若以高频轮询 `pause.toString()` 做反篡改检测,可能在重装间隙检测到变化。性能开销本身不大,但与问题 15 叠加时加重主循环负担。
- **改进建议**:维持 F-B4 重装策略(安全优先),但可优化为:仅当 `probePauseNeutralized() === false`(检测到被还原)时才重装,而非每轮无条件重装。当前代码已有探测逻辑(第 44-55 行),可据此条件化重装。

## 八、storage/storage.js

### 问题 17:`KVS.set`/`KVS.del` 在 IndexedDB 模式下未返回 Promise —— 逻辑错误(API 不一致)

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\foundation\storage\storage.js` 第 47-55 行(`set`)、第 56-59 行(`del`)
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  function set(k, v) {
    var val = String(v);
    if (backendName() !== 'indexeddb') { lsSet(k, val); return; }
    _ready.then(function (ok) { ... });   // ← 未 return Promise
  }
  ```
- **具体表现**:对比 `get`(第 33-46 行)正确 `return _ready.then(...)` 返回 Promise。`set` 和 `del` 在 IndexedDB 分支中调用 `_ready.then(...)` 但**没有 return**,函数返回 `undefined`。
- **后果**:调用方若期望 `KVS.set(k, v).then(...)` 或 `await KVS.set(k, v)`(与 `KVS.get` 的异步语义对称),会得到 `undefined` 并抛 `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'then')`。即使不 await,调用方也无法知道写入是否完成,无法做后续依赖操作。
- **改进建议**:在 `set` 和 `del` 的 IndexedDB 分支前加 `return`:`return _ready.then(function (ok) { ... });`。

### 问题 18:IndexedDB 事务缺少 `onerror`/`oncomplete` 处理 —— 潜在运行时异常(静默丢失)

- **文件**:同上,第 52-53 行
- **问题代码**:
  ```js
  try { var tx = idb.transaction('kv', 'readwrite'); tx.objectStore('kv').put(val, k); }
  catch (e) { lsSet(k, val); }
  ```
- **具体表现**:`catch (e)` 仅捕获事务创建时的同步错误。IndexedDB 写入是异步的,若 `put` 操作在事务提交阶段失败(如配额超限、版本冲突),`onerror` 未注册,错误静默丢失,不会回退到 localStorage。
- **后果**:IndexedDB 写入异步失败时数据丢失且无任何告警/回退,调用方以为写入成功。
- **改进建议**:注册 `tx.onabort`/`tx.onerror` 回调,在失败时 `lsSet(k, val)` 回退并 `swallow`。

### 问题 19:KVS `_ready` 在模块求值期锁定,依赖 CONFIG 加载顺序 —— 潜在运行时异常(时序耦合)

- **文件**:同上,第 32 行
- **问题代码**:`var _ready = (backendName() === 'indexeddb') ? initIDB() : Promise.resolve(false);`
- **具体表现**:`backendName()` 读取 `CONFIG.KVS_BACKEND`。`_ready` 在 storage.js 模块求值时立即求值。若 storage.js 在 config.js 之前求值,`CONFIG` 虽已 hoist 但未赋值,`backendName()` 的 try/catch 会捕获到 `CONFIG` 为 undefined 的错误并返回 `'local'`,导致 `_ready = Promise.resolve(false)`。此后 config.js 加载设置 `CONFIG.KVS_BACKEND = 'indexeddb'` 时,`_ready` 已固定为 resolved false,后续 `get`/`set` 调用 `backendName()` 返回 `'indexeddb'` 进入 IDB 分支,但 `_ready.then(ok => ...)` 中 `ok` 恒为 false,永远回退 localStorage——**IndexedDB 永远不会真正启用**。
- **后果**:若构建拼接顺序变化导致 storage.js 先于 config.js,IndexedDB 后端静默失效,无法察觉。
- **改进建议**:将 `_ready` 改为惰性求值(首次调用 get/set 时才初始化),或在 `_ready` 初始化前显式等待 CONFIG 就绪。

---

## 九、site/site-router.js

### 问题 20:`detectSite()` 每轮多次冗余调用 —— 性能瓶颈(轻微)

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\foundation\site\site-router.js` 第 5-35 行(`detectSite`)与 `dom.js` 第 264 行
- **问题代码**:`walkVideos` 内部第 264 行 `var _taskSel = siteTaskContainerSel();` → `currentSiteCfg()` → `detectSite()`。`walkVideos` 是递归函数,每层递归都重新调用一次。
- **具体表现**:注释(第 96 行)称"脚本单页单站,性价比最高且无需启动期缓存"。但 `detectSite()` 每次执行最多 15 次正则匹配。`walkVideos` 每递归一层(每个 iframe)调用一次 `siteTaskContainerSel()`→`detectSite()`。一个含 10 个 iframe 的页面,单次 `scanVideos` 就触发 10+ 次 `detectSite()`,每次 15 次正则。main-loop.js 第 73 行又直接调用一次。`applyUserRateAll` 虽不调用 detectSite,但与 scanVideos 的遍历叠加。
- **后果**:高频正则匹配叠加全量遍历,在多 iframe 页面上每 2s 产生可测量的 CPU 峰值。
- **改进建议**:用模块级变量缓存 `detectSite()` 结果(站点在单页生命周期内不变),首次调用后直接返回缓存值。

### 问题 21:`renwei` 站点正则仍有误匹配风险 —— 逻辑错误(轻微)

- **文件**:同上,第 25 行
- **问题代码**:`if (/pmphmooc\.com$/i.test(h) || /(^|\.)renwei\.(com|cn)$/i.test(h) || /renwei[-.]/i.test(h)) return 'renwei';`
- **具体表现**:第三段 `/renwei[-.]/i` 匹配任何含 `renwei-` 或 `renwei.` 子串的域名。注释(第 20-24 行)声称已修复 L3 误匹配问题(原 `/renwei/i` 无锚点),但第三段仍可能匹配非人卫域名(如 `myrenwei-shop.com`、`renwei2.example.cn`)。
- **后果**:非人卫站点被误判为 `renwei`,触发人卫专属作答分支,产生非预期 DOM 注入。
- **改进建议**:第三段收紧为 `/(^|\.)renwei[-.]/i` 或仅保留前两段精确匹配,移除宽泛的子串匹配。

---

## 十、bootstrap/main-loop.js(主循环,补充性能问题)

### 问题 22:主循环每轮执行双重全量 DOM 遍历 + `querySelectorAll('*')` —— 性能瓶颈

- **文件**:`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\takeover\bootstrap\main-loop.js` 第 39-40 行、第 69 行;`dom.js` 第 275、280-284 行
- **问题代码**(dom.js walkVideos):
  ```js
  var _sh = root.querySelectorAll('*');                    // 第275行:全量通配符查询
  ...
  var _cts = root.querySelectorAll(_containerSel);
  for (...) {
    var _all = Array.from(_ct.getElementsByTagName('*'));  // 第284行:每个容器再全量遍历
  }
  ```
- **具体表现**:每 2s 一轮的主循环中:
  1. `scanVideos(document)` → `walkVideos` 递归遍历所有 video、shadowRoot、iframe。其中 shadow host 分支执行 `querySelectorAll('*')`(匹配页面所有元素),容器分支对每个匹配容器执行 `getElementsByTagName('*')`。
  2. `applyUserRateAll()` 再独立遍历一次所有 video + iframe(见问题 15)。
  
  两遍全量遍历 + 通配符查询,在大型 DOM 页面上是显著的主线程阻塞点。
- **后果**:页面 DOM 节点多时(如课程列表页),每 2s 出现一次明显的 jank,影响滚动/输入响应。
- **改进建议**:(a) 合并 scanVideos 与 applyUserRateAll(见问题 15);(b) `walkVideos` 中 shadow host 的 `querySelectorAll('*')` 可缩小范围——仅查询可能含 shadowRoot 的自定义元素(如 `[data-v-]`、已知 web component 标签名),而非全量 `*`;(c) 考虑用 MutationObserver 增量扫描替代定时全量扫描。

Let me now also verify the build script's module ordering issue. Looking at the build script's `$domainRel` list:
- `takeover/foundation/utils/utils.js` (line 58) - defines swallow
- `takeover/foundation/state/state.js` (line 60) - defines Store (uses swallow in subs callback)
- ...
- `takeover/bootstrap/main-loop.js` (line 110) - uses Store, scanVideos, etc.

The build script comment says "function declarations hoist, so split/join behavior is identical". This is mostly true for function declarations, but NOT for:
- `var Store = (function(){...})();` - this is a var assignment, not a function declaration. It executes in order. If any module before state.js uses Store, it would fail.
- `var CONFIG = {...}` - same, var assignment
- Top-level immediate function calls

Let me check: does any module BEFORE state.js in the build order use Store? Looking at the order:
1. utils.js (swallow, dbg, cxState)
2. url.js (topHref, urlParam)
3. state.js (Store) <- Store defined here
4. metrics.js
5. config.js (CONFIG) <- CONFIG defined here
6. styles.js (STYLES, __cxUI)
7. storage.js (uses CONFIG, DEBUG - both defined after! Wait, storage.js is at position 7, config.js at position 5. So CONFIG is defined before storage.js. Good.)

Wait, but styles.js is at position 6, and it says "在 presentation/dom 读取前定义". Let me check if styles.js uses Store. Actually, looking at the grep results, Store.emit is used extensively but Store is defined early (position 3 in the list). So by the time any function that uses Store is CALLED (at runtime, not load time), Store is already defined.

The key insight is: var assignments execute in order at load time, but function declarations hoist. So as long as:
1. All var assignments that functions depend on are executed before those functions are CALLED (not just defined)
2. No immediate top-level code uses a var that hasn't been assigned yet

The main-loop.js has top-level immediate statements:
- `try { installPlayWatch(document); }` (line 28) - calls installPlayWatch, which uses _playWatchDocs (defined in lifecycle.js, which comes AFTER main-loop.js in build order!)
- `try { hookAttachments(); }` - uses targeting.js functions
- `try { bridgeInit(); }` - uses bridge.js functions  
- `try { refreshTargets(); }` - uses targeting.js
- `try { scanVideos(document); neutralizeGlobalPause(window); }` - uses dom.js
- `var _loopTimer = null;` then `try { _loopTimer = setInterval(_loopTick, CONFIG.RESCAN_INTERVAL); }` - uses CONFIG (defined in config.js, position 5, before main-loop.js position 110)
- `Store.state.TARGET = TARGET;` etc. - uses Store (position 3), TARGET (defined in targeting.js, position 8)

Wait, but main-loop.js is the LAST module in the build order (position 110 in $domainRel). So all other modules' var assignments have already executed. Let me verify:

Now, let me also check the `_loopTimer` stale mirror issue more carefully. The state.js comment at lines 7-8 explicitly says:
"对基本类型（如 _loopTimer 重启后的新 id）镜像不会自动同步，需在赋值处一并更新镜像"

So this is a KNOWN, DOCUMENTED limitation. But it's still a real issue - after panel-driven restart (lines 5947-5948) or /rescan command (lines 7077-7078), Store.state._loopTimer becomes stale (points to the old, cleared timer id). The diagnostics page would show a stale timer id. This is a minor diagnostic issue, not a functional bug.

Now let me also check: is there a "main loop stops" risk? The _loopTick function:
- Each section is wrapped in try/catch
- setInterval callbacks that throw don't stop the interval
- So the loop should never "stop" unless clearInterval is called

The only places clearInterval is called:
1. lifecycle.js cleanupListeners (line 60) - on page unload
2. panel-controls.js (line 5947) - on RESCAN_INTERVAL change, then immediately restarts
3. commands.js (line 7077) - on /rescan command, then immediately restarts

So the loop won't unexpectedly stop. Good.

But there's a subtle issue: at line 5948 and 7078, `try { _loopTimer = setInterval(_loopTick, CONFIG.RESCAN_INTERVAL); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }` - if setInterval throws (extremely unlikely), _loopTimer would still hold the cleared (old) value, and the loop would be dead. But this is extremely unlikely.

More importantly: at line 5947, `try { if (_loopTimer) clearInterval(_loopTimer); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }` - if clearInterval throws, _loopTimer is NOT cleared, and the next line sets _loopTimer to a new interval. Now there are TWO intervals running! This is a potential double-tick issue. But again, clearInterval rarely throws.

Actually, wait - there's a more subtle issue. At line 5947:
```
try { if (_loopTimer) clearInterval(_loopTimer); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
try { _loopTimer = setInterval(_loopTick, CONFIG.RESCAN_INTERVAL); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
```
If the first try succeeds (clearInterval works), but the second try fails (setInterval throws), then _loopTimer is still the old (now cleared) value. The loop is dead. But setInterval basically never throws.

If the first try fails (clearInterval throws - very rare), _loopTimer still holds the old value. The second try creates a new interval. Now there are two intervals (the old one wasn't actually cleared because clearInterval threw). Double-ticking. But again, extremely rare.

These are very edge-casey. Let me focus on the more impactful issues.

Let me now also check the _sim/sim-idempotency.js test more carefully. It checks:
1. First injection no error
2. started=true
3. No double setInterval (idempotent guard works)
4. Second injection no error

But the test uses `window.eval(code)` to inject. The second eval should hit the `if (window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.started) return;` guard. This is correct.

However, I notice the test at line 61: `assert('场景1 第二次注入不抛异常（因 started 跳过）', injectErr === null, ...)` - but injectErr is set on BOTH the first and second injection. If the first injection succeeds, injectErr stays null. If the second injection is skipped by the guard (return), it also doesn't throw. So injectErr should be null. This is correct.

But wait - the test reuses `injectErr` variable. At line 52: `try { window.eval(code); } catch (e) { injectErr = e; }` - first injection.
At line 54: `try { window.eval(code); } catch (e) { injectErr = (injectErr ? injectErr + '; ' : '') + e; }` - second injection.

If the first injection succeeds, injectErr is null. The second injection, if it throws, would set injectErr to the error. But if the guard works (return early), no throw, injectErr stays null. This is fine.

Now let me also check: the sim-idempotency test has a potential issue. At line 39: `window.addEventListener('error', (e) => errors.push(...))`. And at line 126: `assert('全局 无运行时错误', errors.length === 0, errors.slice(0, 3))`. This asserts NO runtime errors at all. But the artifact might produce some errors in jsdom (which doesn't fully support all browser APIs). The test suppresses "Not implemented:" errors at line 41, but other errors would cause this assertion to fail. This is a brittle test.

Now, let me also look at the sim-lifecycle.js test. It uses `runScripts: 'outside-only'` and `vm.runInContext`. It checks that uninstall() removes global symbols. This looks correct.

But there's a potential issue: at line 42: `assert('命名空间已安装', injected)`. If the artifact fails to inject in jsdom (which is likely since jsdom doesn't support many APIs), `injected` would be false, and all subsequent assertions would be skipped. The test would still pass (0 failures) because it only runs assertions if `injected` is true. This is a weak test - it could silently pass even if injection fails.

Actually, looking at line 28-34:
```
let injected = false;
try {
  vm.runInContext(artifact, ctx, { filename: 'artifact.user.js' });
  injected = !!window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY;
} catch (e) {
  console.log('INJECT_NOTE — ...');
}
```
If the artifact throws during injection, `injected` stays false, and the test passes with 0 failures (since the `if (injected)` block is skipped). The only assertion that could fail is `assert('命名空间已安装', injected)` at line 42. If injected is false, this would be 1 FAIL. So the test does catch injection failure. Good.

Now let me also check the build script for the module ordering issue with lifecycle.js. Looking at the build script $domainRel:
- Line 94: `'takeover/dom/dom.js'`
- Line 95: `'takeover/dom/session.js'`
- Line 96: `'presentation/toast.js'`

This runs at load time. At this point:

- `_loopTimer` is referenced inside cleanupListeners but only at call time - OK (it's a var declared in main-loop.js, which runs later, but cleanupListeners is only CALLED on pagehide/unload, by which time main-loop.js has run)

Actually wait - `_loopTimer` is declared with `var _loopTimer = null;` at line 106 of main-loop.js (artifact line 7554). Due to var hoisting, `_loopTimer` is hoisted to the top of the IIFE as `var _loopTimer;` (undefined). It's assigned `null` at line 106, and then assigned the setInterval result at line 108. So if cleanupListeners is called before main-loop.js runs (which won't happen since pagehide/beforeunload are page lifecycle events that fire after load), _loopTimer would be undefined, and `if (_loopTimer)` would be false, so clearInterval wouldn't be called. 我发现 zhihuishu-fab.js 中有几个问题：

1. 第 137-145 行和第 187-195 行：`pop.querySelector('#__cxZheAns').addEventListener(...)` — 这里没有对 querySelector 的结果做 null 守卫。如果 `_zhsExamPopHTML()` 返回的 HTML 有问题，或者 DOM 还没渲染好，querySelector 返回 null，调用 addEventListener 会抛 TypeError。但被外层 try/catch 处理了。不过 `_zhsMaybeSwitchMode` 第 137 行其实没有外层 try/catch！让我再看一下。

实际上第 136-145 行在 `_zhsMaybeSwitchMode` 函数内，这个函数本身没有 try/catch，但调用方 `_zhsRefreshFab`（第 123 行）有 try/catch。然而 `_zhsMaybeSwitchMode` 是从 `_zhsEnsureFab`（第 164 行）调用的，而 `_zhsEnsureFab` 又在 `zhihuishuFabTick`（第 214 行）的 try/catch 中。所以最终是安全的。但如果在 `_zhsMaybeSwitchMode` 内部抛错，会导致后续的 `_zhsRefreshFab()` 不执行。

2. 第 220 行：`try { window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.zhihuishuFabTick = zhihuishuFabTick; } catch (e) {}` — 这里假设 `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY` 已存在。如果不存在，访问 `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.zhihuishuFabTick` 会抛 TypeError，被 catch 了，但这意味着 `zhihuishuFabTick` 不会被暴露，主循环可能无法调用它。这是个潜在问题。

3. 第 174 行 `pd.body.appendChild(fab);` — 如果 `pd.body` 为 null（文档未就绪），会抛错。被外层 try/catch 处理。

4. 第 163 行：`if (detectSite() !== 'zhihuishu') return;` — `detectSite` 没有 typeof 守卫，如果未定义会抛 ReferenceError。但它是闭包内的函数，应该已定义。

5. 第 178 行：`pop.innerHTML = _zhsIsExamPage() ? _zhsExamPopHTML() : _zhsVideoPopHTML();` — 用 innerHTML 注入 HTML，这是热路径（每次切换模式都调用，但不是高频）。可接受。

6. 第 173 行：`fab.innerHTML = ZHS_FAB_SVG + '<span class="cx-zhs-dot"></span>';` — innerHTML 注入，一次性，可接受。

7. 第 152 行：`window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.CONFIG.INTRUSION_MODE = _zhsFabEnabled ? 'auto' : 'gentle';` — 这里直接修改 `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.CONFIG.INTRUSION_MODE`，但本地 CONFIG 是闭包内的，这两个 CONFIG 是否同步？这取决于 `window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.CONFIG` 是否就是闭包内的 CONFIG 引用。如果不是，这里修改不会影响本地 CONFIG，导致状态不一致。这是一个潜在的逻辑错误。

8. 第 138 行：`if (window.__cxRegisterAddon) window.__cxRegisterAddon();` — 调用 registerAddon 但不传参数，可能重新注册所有 addon。

回到 panel.js 第 28-31 行的 syncPanelInputs：
```js
var auto = _cxPanel.querySelector('#__cxAuto'); if (auto) { auto.value = CONFIG.AUTO_STOP_MIN; _cxPanel.querySelector('#__cxAutoVal').textContent = CONFIG.AUTO_STOP_MIN; }
var res = _cxPanel.querySelector('#__cxResume'); if (res) { res.value = CONFIG.RESUME_AFTER_MIN; _cxPanel.querySelector('#__cxResumeVal').textContent = CONFIG.RESUME_AFTER_MIN; }
```

这里如果 `#__cxAutoVal` 不存在，`_cxPanel.querySelector('#__cxAutoVal').textContent` 会抛 TypeError（null 解引用）。虽然被外层 try/catch 处理，但会导致后续的 `res`/`resc`/`endrel` 行不执行。这是一个潜在问题——一个缺失的元素会导致整个 syncPanelInputs 中断。

让我再确认一下 panel-controls.js 的 quizAuto 重复声明：
- 第 45 行：`var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAuto');` — 这是「自动答题」开关
- 第 174 行：`var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAutoSubmit');` — 这是「作答后自动提交」开关

由于 var 提升，实际上 `quizAuto` 在函数作用域内只有一个绑定。执行顺序：
1. 行 45：`quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAuto')` → 绑定到 #__cxQuizAuto 元素
2. 行 46-92：在该 if 块内使用 quizAuto（绑定到 #__cxQuizAuto），绑定 change 事件
3. 行 174：`quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAutoSubmit')` → 重新赋值为 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 元素
4. 行 175-183：在该 if 块内使用 quizAuto（现在绑定到 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit），绑定 change 事件

由于事件监听器是在各自元素上绑定的，元素引用本身不会被覆盖影响（监听器已经注册到正确的元素上）。所以功能上目前可能没 bug。但这是一个非常危险的代码：
- 如果在第 92 行到 174 行之间有任何代码引用 `quizAuto` 期望它是 #__cxQuizAuto，会得到错误的对象。
- 可读性极差，维护者会困惑。
- 第 87-91 行的 change 回调里用 `quizAuto.checked`，由于闭包，它引用的是变量 `quizAuto`，到第 174 行后 quizAuto 已变成 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 元素！这意味着如果用户在 #__cxQuizAuto 上切换，回调里 `quizAuto.checked` 读的是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 的 checked 状态，而不是被切换的 #__cxQuizAuto！

让我仔细验证这一点。第 87-91 行：
```js
quizAuto.addEventListener('change', function () {
  try { if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') localStorage.setItem('cx_quiz_auto', quizAuto.checked ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
  try { Store.emit('ui:toast', quizAuto.checked ? '通用自动答题已开启' : '通用自动答题已关闭（当前页正在作答会停在本页）'); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
  Store.emit('panel:refresh');   // 联动刷新「做题」区进度显示
});
```

这里 `quizAuto` 是闭包变量。当 change 事件触发时（用户切换 #__cxQuizAuto 复选框），回调执行时读取 `quizAuto.checked`。此时 `quizAuto` 变量的值是什么？由于 var 提升，整个函数作用域内 `quizAuto` 是同一个变量。到第 174 行执行后，`quizAuto` 已被重新赋值为 `el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAutoSubmit')`。所以回调里 `quizAuto.checked` 读的是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 的 checked 状态，而不是用户实际切换的 #__cxQuizAuto！这是一个真实的 bug！

具体后果：用户在面板上勾选/取消「自动答题（#__cxQuizAuto）」时，写入 localStorage 的值取决于「作答后自动提交（#__cxQuizAutoSubmit）」的勾选状态，而不是用户实际操作的开关。如果两个开关状态不一致，会导致配置写反。同样第 175-182 行的 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 的 change 回调里 `quizAuto.checked` 读的也是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit（因为此时 quizAuto 已被赋值为它），这个反而是对的——但这是巧合。

等等，让我再想想。第 174 行 `var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAutoSubmit');` 执行后，quizAuto 指向 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit。之后第 177-182 行的回调里 `quizAuto.checked` 读的是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit，这是正确的（因为回调就是绑定在 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 上）。

但第 87-91 行的回调（绑定在 #__cxQuizAuto 上）里 `quizAuto.checked` 读的是 quizAuto 变量的当前值。由于第 174 行在绑定回调之后执行（第 174 行在第 87 行之后），所以当第 174 行执行完毕，quizAuto 已指向 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit。此后用户触发 #__cxQuizAuto 的 change 事件时，回调读 `quizAuto.checked` 读的是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 的状态。

这是一个确定的 bug！用户操作「自动答题」开关时，写入的 localStorage 值来自「自动提交」开关的状态。

让我确认执行顺序：
- bindPanelControlEvents(el) 被调用
- 行 8: var intr = ...
- 行 34: var polite = ...
- 行 45: var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAuto'); // quizAuto → #__cxQuizAuto
- 行 46: if (quizAuto) {  // true
- 行 87: quizAuto.addEventListener('change', function() { ... quizAuto.checked ... }); // 绑定回调，回调内引用 quizAuto 变量
- 行 92: } // end if
- 行 94: var popupAuto = ...
- 行 107: var quizSrc = ...
- 行 121: var quizOffline = ...
- 行 132: var quizCdn = ...
- 行 145: var quizDs = ...
- 行 174: var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAutoSubmit'); // 重复声明，quizAuto → #__cxQuizAutoSubmit
- 行 175: if (quizAuto) {  // true
- 行 177: quizAuto.addEventListener('change', function() { ... quizAuto.checked ... }); // 绑定回调到 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit
- 行 183: } // end if

所以确认：第 87 行的回调在用户触发时会读 quizAuto（此时已是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit）的 checked。这是 bug。

这是一个确定的逻辑错误，值得重点报告。

让我再检查一下其他几个潜在问题：

panel-core.js 第 115 行：
```js
if (c.args) { cmdInp.value = '/' + c.name + ' '; try { cmdInp.focus(); var L = cmdInp.value.length; cmdInp.setSelectionRange(L, L); } catch (ee) { swallow(ee); } _cxCmdRender(_cxCmdFilter, idx); }
```
这里 `var L` 在 if 块内，但 var 提升到函数作用域。无害。

panel-core.js 第 134 行：
```js
var vv = (_lastVideoList && _lastVideoList[idx]) || allVideos()[idx];
```
如果 `_lastVideoList[idx]` 是 undefined 但 `_lastVideoList` 存在，会回退到 `allVideos()[idx]`。这是合理的。

panel-core.js 第 22 行：
```js
var _cxBuildLater = function () { if (!_cxPanel && pd.body) ensurePanel(); };
```
递归调用 ensurePanel。如果 pd.body 一直不存在，会一直返回 null，不会死循环。但如果 readyState 是 'loading'，会等 DOMContentLoaded。如果 DOMContentLoaded 已经触发了（addEventListener 后才触发），那 _cxBuildLater 不会被调用。但如果是 loading 状态，DOMContentLoaded 会触发 _cxBuildOnce → _cxBuildLater → ensurePanel。看起来没问题。

panel-drag.js 第 20-21 行：
```js
nx = Math.max(0, Math.min(nx, window.innerWidth - w));   // w/h 已在 mousedown 缓存
ny = Math.max(0, Math.min(ny, Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - h)));
```
这里用 `window.innerWidth/innerHeight`，但如果面板挂在 window.top（跨 iframe），拖拽时用的是当前 frame 的 window 尺寸，而不是 top 的尺寸。这可能导致拖拽范围计算错误。不过 panel-core.js 第 13 行说面板挂在 top.document，但拖拽监听是在当前 frame 的 document 上。这可能是设计如此。

panel-drag.js 第 61 行：
```js
document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true });
```
passive: true 但 onMove 不调 preventDefault，没问题。

panel-ds-console.js 第 30 行：
```js
window.__cxDsTicker = setInterval(function () { try { _dsUpdateConsole(el); } catch (e) { swallow(e); } }, 2000);
```
全局 ticker 没有清理。如果脚本被卸载或页面 SPA 切换，ticker 会一直跑。但用户脚本一般不卸载。另外 `el` 是闭包捕获的，如果面板被重建，el 会指向旧的已分离节点，_dsUpdateConsole 会刷新一个不在 DOM 中的节点，浪费 CPU。这是一个潜在的内存泄漏/性能问题。

panel-ds-console.js 第 14 行：
```js
if (window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY) window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY.DS_RESPONDER_ENABLED = enabled;
```
没有 typeof 守卫，但用 `if (window.__CX_FORCE_PLAY)` 守卫了，安全。

dashboard.js 第 94-95 行：
```js
var on = (_qa = (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') ? localStorage.getItem('cx_quiz_auto') : null, _qa !== '0');   // 与 controls 默认开一致
var _qa;
```
在声明前使用 _qa。由于 var 提升，_qa 是 undefined，然后被赋值为 localStorage.getItem 的结果。这能工作但极不规范。逗号表达式 + 赋值 + 声明后置，可读性极差。

dashboard.js 第 96 行：
```js
if (!st || !st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0) {
```
运算符优先级：`&&` 高于 `||`，所以等价于 `(!st) || (!st.scanned && st.scanned !== 0)`。逻辑上：st 不存在，或 st.scanned 是 falsy 但不是 0。这应该是想判断"未扫描"。逻辑正确但可读性差，应加括号。

dashboard.js 第 277 行：
```js
wrap.innerHTML = html;
```
renderVideoList 在每次 panel:refresh（节流 150ms）时调用，用 innerHTML 全量重建视频列表 DOM。这是热路径上的 innerHTML 操作，性能不佳。对于视频数量多的页面，会有明显卡顿。而且会破坏用户当前的交互状态（如 hover）。

dashboard.js 第 197 行：
```js
var max = Math.max.apply(null, hist) || 1, n = hist.length;
```
如果 hist 全是 0，max=0，`|| 1` 兜底为 1。没问题。

panel-template.js 第 57 行：
```js
try { if (typeof detectSite === 'function' && detectSite() === 'deepseek') return buildDSConsoleHTML(_inFrame); } catch (e) {}
```
catch 吞掉错误，但如果没有 return，会继续执行下面的 buildPanelHTML 主体。这是故意的降级。

commands.js 第 85 行：
```js
for (var i = localStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
```
直接访问 localStorage，如果 localStorage 抛异常（无痕模式某些情况），被外层 try/catch 处理。

commands.js 第 127 行：
```js
toggleNinjaMode(); Store.emit('ui:toast', 'n 模式已' + (CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开'));
```
toggleNinjaMode 切换后，CONFIG.NINJA_MODE 是新值。如果原本关→开，CONFIG.NINJA_MODE=true，toast 显示 `'关'`？这是错的！等等，让我再看。

`'n 模式已' + (CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开')` — 如果切换后 CONFIG.NINJA_MODE=true（已开），toast 显示 `'n 模式已关'`？这是反的！

等等，让我再仔细看。toggleNinjaMode 会切换 ninja 复选框并触发 change 事件，change 事件里会设置 `CONFIG.NINJA_MODE = !!ninja.checked`。所以 toggleNinjaMode 后 CONFIG.NINJA_MODE 是新值。

如果原本是关(false)，toggle 后变开(true)。toast 应显示"n 模式已开"。但代码是 `CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开'` = `true ? '关' : '开'` = `'关'`。显示"n 模式已关"，这是错的！

如果原本是开(true)，toggle 后变关(false)。toast 应显示"n 模式已关"。代码 `CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开'` = `false ? '关' : '开'` = `'开'`。显示"n 模式已开"，这也是错的！

所以 commands.js 第 127 行的 toast 文案是反的！这是一个确定的逻辑错误。

不过等等，让我再看 arg 处理：
```js
if (arg) { var on = !/^off$/i.test(arg.trim()); if (!!CONFIG.NINJA_MODE === on) { Store.emit('ui:toast', 'n 模式已是 ' + (on ? 'on' : 'off')); return; } }
toggleNinjaMode(); Store.emit('ui:toast', 'n 模式已' + (CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开'));
```
如果带了 arg 且当前状态已是目标状态，提前 return。否则 toggleNinjaMode() 切换，然后 toast。

确认：toggleNinjaMode 后 CONFIG.NINJA_MODE 是切换后的新值。toast 的三元表达式是反的。应该是 `(CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '开' : '关')`。这是 bug。

让我再检查 panel.js 的 syncPanelInputs 第 28-31 行的潜在 null 解引用。实际上 `_cxPanel.querySelector('#__cxAutoVal')` 如果返回 null，`.textContent` 会抛 TypeError。这在 try/catch 内，但会中断 syncPanelInputs 后续语句。不过由于面板模板是固定的，#__cxAutoVal 总是存在，所以实际不会触发。但这是防御性编程的缺失。

panel-core.js 第 91 行：
```js
var closeBtn = el.querySelector('#__cxPanelClose'); if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', hidePanel);   // DS 控制台无此按钮，需 null 守卫
```
有 null 守卫，好。

panel-core.js 第 247 行：
```js
if (!_wasOpen) { try { Store.emit('panel:refresh'); } catch (e2) { swallow(e2); } }   // 折叠→展开：刷新动态内容
```
注释说"折叠→展开"，但 `!_wasOpen` 意味着"之前不是 open（即折叠）"，toggle 后变成 open，所以是"折叠→展开"。正确。

我还需要检查一个事情：panel-controls.js 第 174 行的 quizAuto 重复声明是否真的会影响第 87 行的回调。让我再确认一下 JavaScript 的 var 提升和闭包语义。

```js
function bindPanelControlEvents(el) {
  // ...
  var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAuto');  // 行 45
  if (quizAuto) {
    // ...
    quizAuto.addEventListener('change', function () {
      // 这里 quizAuto 是闭包变量，引用的是函数作用域内的 quizAuto
      localStorage.setItem('cx_quiz_auto', quizAuto.checked ? '1' : '0');
    });
  }
  // ...
  var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAutoSubmit');  // 行 174，重复声明
  if (quizAuto) {
    // ...
    quizAuto.addEventListener('change', function () {
      localStorage.setItem('cx_quiz_autosubmit', quizAuto.checked ? '1' : '0');
    });
  }
}
```

由于 `var quizAuto` 提升，整个函数作用域内只有一个 `quizAuto` 变量。两个回调闭包捕获的是同一个变量。

执行顺序：
1. 行 45：quizAuto = #__cxQuizAuto 元素
2. 行 87：给 #__cxQuizAuto 绑定 change 回调（回调内引用 quizAuto 变量）
3. 行 174：quizAuto = #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 元素（重新赋值）
4. 行 177：给 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 绑定 change 回调（回调内引用 quizAuto 变量）

当用户之后切换 #__cxQuizAuto（触发第 87 行的回调）时，回调读取 `quizAuto.checked`。此时 quizAuto 变量的值是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 元素（第 174 行赋值的结果）。所以回调读取的是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 的 checked，而不是 #__cxQuizAuto 的 checked。

确认：这是真实的 bug。用户切换"自动答题"开关时，写入 localStorage 的值取决于"自动提交"开关的状态。

后果：
- 如果用户开启"自动答题"但"自动提交"是关的，localStorage.cx_quiz_auto 会被写入 '0'（因为 quizAuto.checked 读的是 #__cxQuizAutoSubmit 的 false），导致自动答题实际被关闭。
- 反之亦然。

这是一个严重的状态不一致 bug。

好，现在我有足够的发现了。让我写报告。

让我再仔细确认 commands.js 第 127 行的 bug。
```js
registerCommand('ninja', '切换 n 模式（可加 on/off）', true, function (raw, arg) {
  try { if (arg) { var on = !/^off$/i.test(arg.trim()); if (!!CONFIG.NINJA_MODE === on) { Store.emit('ui:toast', 'n 模式已是 ' + (on ? 'on' : 'off')); return; } }
    toggleNinjaMode(); Store.emit('ui:toast', 'n 模式已' + (CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开'));
  } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
});
```

场景1：用户输入 `/ninja`（无参数），当前 NINJA_MODE=false（关）
- arg 为空，跳过 if
- toggleNinjaMode() 切换 → NINJA_MODE 变 true（开）
- toast: `'n 模式已' + (true ? '关' : '开')` = `'n 模式已关'`
- 但实际已开启！文案反了。

场景2：用户输入 `/ninja`（无参数），当前 NINJA_MODE=true（开）
- arg 为空，跳过 if
- toggleNinjaMode() 切换 → NINJA_MODE 变 false（关）
- toast: `'n 模式已' + (false ? '关' : '开')` = `'n 模式已开'`
- 但实际已关闭！文案反了。

确认：这是 bug。toast 三元表达式的两个分支写反了。应该是 `(CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '开' : '关')`。

现在写最终报告。我用中文。

让我整理一下所有发现，确保不遗漏。

再检查一下 panel-core.js 第 348 行：
```js
switchTab(_cxActiveTab || 'control');   // 主从式导航：应用上次选中的区块（默认 主控）
```
_cxActiveTab 在 panel.js 第 15 行初始化为 'control'，所以 `|| 'control'` 是冗余但无害。

panel-core.js 第 300 行 switchTab 是函数声明，在 ensurePanel 内部。它被闭包捕获。第 327 行的 Store.onEv 回调也引用 switchTab。由于 switchTab 是函数声明，hoist 到 ensurePanel 作用域顶部。OK。

但有一个问题：switchTab 是 ensurePanel 内的局部函数，第 327 行 `Store.onEv('ui:switchTab', function (name) { ... switchTab(name) ... })` 注册了一个事件监听。如果 ensurePanel 被调用多次（虽然第 9 行有 `if (_cxPanel) return` 守卫，不会重复执行），但如果 _cxPanel 被清除后重建，会重复注册 ui:switchTab 监听。不过 _cxPanel 一般不清除。这是低风险问题。

panel.js 第 57-62 行：
```js
try { Store.onEv('ui:toast', toast); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
try { Store.onEv('panel:refresh', refreshPanelState); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
try { Store.onEv('videos:scanned', throttle(refreshPanelState, 150)); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
```
这些监听在模块加载时注册一次。如果模块被多次加载（iframe 多次注入），每个 frame 都会注册。但 Store 可能是 frame 级的，所以 OK。

panel.js 第 84 行：
```js
try { Store.onEv('panel:refresh', renderInvasionReport); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
try { Store.onEv('videos:scanned', throttle(renderInvasionReport, 1500)); } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
```
renderInvasionReport 也被 panel:refresh 触发。这意味着每次 panel:refresh 会触发两次 renderInvasionReport（一次直接，一次通过 refreshPanelState？不，refreshPanelState 不调 renderInvasionReport）。实际上 panel:refresh 触发 refreshPanelState 和 renderInvasionReport 两个监听。这不重复，但都执行。OK。

但注意：panel.js 第 58 行 `Store.onEv('panel:refresh', refreshPanelState)` 和第 84 行 `Store.onEv('panel:refresh', renderInvasionReport)` 都订阅了 panel:refresh。如果 Store.onEv 支持多订阅，没问题。如果只保留最后一个，则 refreshPanelState 会被覆盖。这取决于 Store 实现。从代码看应该支持多订阅。

好，我写报告了。

实际上 _zhsMaybeSwitchMode（第 127-160 行）和 _zhsEnsureFab（第 162-209 行）中的 exam 分支代码高度重复：
- 第 136-145 行（_zhsMaybeSwitchMode 内）与第 178 行 + 186-195 行（_zhsEnsureFab 内）几乎相同。

# 代码审查报告：presentation/ 目录

## 一、panel-controls.js

### 问题 1.1【严重·逻辑错误】`quizAuto` 重复声明导致 change 回调读取错误元素

- 文件：`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\panel-controls.js`
- 行号：第 45 行 与 第 174 行（重复 `var quizAuto`）；受影响回调在第 87-91 行

问题代码：
```js
// 行 45
var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAuto');        // 指向「自动答题」开关
...
// 行 87-91（绑定在 #__cxQuizAuto 上的回调）
quizAuto.addEventListener('change', function () {
  localStorage.setItem('cx_quiz_auto', quizAuto.checked ? '1' : '0');   // 闭包引用 quizAuto 变量
  Store.emit('ui:toast', quizAuto.checked ? '通用自动答题已开启' : '...');
});
...
// 行 174（重复声明，覆盖同一函数作用域变量）
var quizAuto = el.querySelector('#__cxQuizAutoSubmit');  // 指向「作答后自动提交」开关
```

具体表现：由于 `var` 提升到 `bindPanelControlEvents` 函数作用域顶部，两处 `var quizAuto` 是同一个绑定。执行到第 174 行后 `quizAuto` 被重新赋值为 `#__cxQuizAutoSubmit` 元素。此后用户勾选「自动答题」(`#__cxQuizAuto`) 触发第 87 行回调时，回调闭包读到的 `quizAuto.checked` 是 `#__cxQuizAutoSubmit` 的勾选状态，而非用户实际操作的开关。

可能导致的后果：用户切换「自动答题」开关时，写入 `localStorage.cx_quiz_auto` 的值取决于「自动提交」开关的状态，两个开关状态不一致时配置写反，自动答题引擎的闸门(`cx_quiz_auto`)与用户意图相悖，表现为"勾了自动答题却不工作"或"关了却还在答"。

改进建议：将第 174 行变量改名为 `quizAutoSubmit`，消除重复声明；或整体改用 `let`/`const`（重复声明会直接报错，便于发现）。

### 问题 1.2【中·可维护性】`bindPanelControlEvents` 单函数过长

- 行号：第 5-256 行，约 252 行

具体表现：单个函数内串联了入侵模式、礼貌模式、自动答题、弹窗作答、答案来源、离线、CDN 授权、DeepSeek、自动提交、人工复核、黑匣子导出/清空等十余个独立控件的初始化与事件绑定。

后果：阅读、定位、回归测试困难，新增控件易误改其它分支（如问题 1.1 就被这种长函数掩盖）。

改进建议：按控件域拆分为 `_bindQuizControls(el)`、`_bindIntrusionControls(el)`、`_bindBlackboxControls(el)` 等子函数。

### 问题 1.3【低·可维护性】多处空 `catch` 静默吞错

- 行号：第 86 行 `} catch (e) {}`、第 127 行、第 168 行

具体表现：项目其它地方统一用 `swallow(e)` 记录遥测，但这几处直接空 catch，调试时无法在 `_errBuf` 中看到对应错误。

改进建议：统一改为 `catch (e) { swallow(e); }`。

---

## 二、commands.js

### 问题 2.1【严重·逻辑错误】`/ninja` 命令 toast 文案与实际状态相反

- 文件：`c:\Users\24033_1dhcyji\CodeBuddy\20260723173246\ecosystem\src\chaoxing-force-play\presentation\commands.js`
- 行号：第 125-129 行，焦点在第 127 行

问题代码：
```js
registerCommand('ninja', '切换 n 模式（可加 on/off）', true, function (raw, arg) {
  try { if (arg) { var on = !/^off$/i.test(arg.trim()); if (!!CONFIG.NINJA_MODE === on) { Store.emit('ui:toast', 'n 模式已是 ' + (on ? 'on' : 'off')); return; } }
    toggleNinjaMode(); Store.emit('ui:toast', 'n 模式已' + (CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开'));
                                                                                   // ^^^ 三元两个分支写反
  } catch (e) { swallow(e); }
});
```

具体表现：`toggleNinjaMode()` 执行后 `CONFIG.NINJA_MODE` 已是新值。三元表达式 `(CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '关' : '开')` 的两个分支写反了——开启后 `NINJA_MODE=true` 却输出"关"，关闭后输出"开"。

后果：用户执行 `/ninja` 后看到与实际相反的提示，误以为命令失效，反复触发导致状态在开/关之间来回跳。

改进建议：改为 `(CONFIG.NINJA_MODE ? '开' : '关')`。

### 问题 2.2【低·可维护性】`_cxCmdShowAll` 冗余判断

- 行号：第 95 行 `_cxCmdShowAll && _cxCmdShowAll();`

具体表现：`_cxCmdShowAll` 是同作用域函数声明（第 195 行 `function _cxCmdShowAll()`），hoist 后恒为函数引用，`&&` 判断永远为真。

改进建议：直接 `_cxCmdShowAll();`，或若顾虑被覆盖则改用 `typeof _cxCmdShowAll === 'function'` 守卫。
